## Final-window cache and rewriting evidence — 14 October 2026

- Audit window: Monday 12 October 00:00 to Wednesday 14 October 09:54:11 Dubai. Investigation only; no production changes. Coverage is tracked in [the concluding audit](output/platform-audit-2026-10-14/coverage.md).
- **H-032, confirmed measurement:** 4,392 main model usage events in this window contain 755,271,315 input tokens and 134,479,572 reported cache-read tokens: **17.81% token coverage**. At least one cached token appears on 87.41% of calls; that is a different, misleadingly reassuring metric. Requested-model coverage: DeepSeek 22.56%, Qwen 10.41%, MiniMax 37.03%. Actual served-provider attribution is missing, so backend-specific attribution remains unresolved.
- **H-032, confirmed code contributor:** MCP discovery changes the next-call schema set; persisted connector usage can reveal the whole admitted server, not only the requested tool. Name sorting is already deterministic and does not solve set changes. Dynamic grounding, recovery/runtime text and activated skills also change early system content.
- **H-032, separate unexplained loss:** 394 drops in 126 runs / 81 tasks occur with unchanged tracked tools/system/prior-message hashes, nonshrinking input and a gap of at most 60 seconds. These are evidence against blaming all losses on tool discovery. Hashes stop before gateway transformation/provider token rendering; investigate those layers and cache routing/retention. No short TTL is proven.
- **H-002, new confirmed polish defect:** task `f287235b-34ee-402f-8af8-bf779256c2b1`, run `e558ef80-86c6-49fe-93e5-9f33ff765595` (13 October): a personal responsibility phrase was changed from first-person singular to plural, explicitly to match the surrounding company voice. Preserve clause-level pronouns, agency, facts and modality; general style consistency must not override this. Healthy controls include `69843ff8-2c18-4708-b654-d822c18fa355` and `dd92936c-8118-4c5e-9a36-9798795f80ea`. Ordinary polishing should return the draft with minimal commentary.
- Current-window evidence and the earlier partial-month findings are consolidated in [the report](output/platform-audit-2026-10-14/report.md). Source/native-artifact checks remain distinct from workflow review.


## Proposed Berry system prompt — 14 October 2026

At the user's request, a review draft is saved in [SYSTEM_PROMPT.md](SYSTEM_PROMPT.md), with [design, cache integration and evaluation notes](output/platform-audit-2026-10-14/system-prompt-design.md). This is documentation only; no prompt, application, configuration or production deployment was changed.

**H-002 / H-022:** The existing local and captured deployed rewriting prompt are byte-identical (3,896 characters; SHA-256 de72fe1a79f0dc81c18f34fd2cb6dbe9266c019c6d24b2515c2af74b2e9f6255). It already protects first-person voice, facts, qualifications and latest corrections. The proposal makes chat user/draft author/incoming sender/recipient distinctions explicit, preserves I/we clause by clause, adds a draft-language default and tests style-only versus explicitly substantive edits. An absent explicit language rule is a prompt gap; no platform-wide translation failure rate is inferred. Task f287235b-34ee-402f-8af8-bf779256c2b1 proves personal-to-company agency drift; task 2f87509d-5462-4414-9983-0ad55d9cc312 supplies another polishing example with added promotional certainty. Do not count this proposal as a validated or deployed remedy.

**Harness boundary:** The useful pattern in the user-provided DeepSeek example is concrete identity/environment/tool contracts; its local paths, UI URL, model branding, goals and delegation machinery are not portable instructions. The proposed Berry prompt uses existing tool names and keeps changing actor/environment/writing state out of its static text. Integration must verify actual gateway serialization: a second system-role message can still be prepended ahead of history. This does not fix changing MCP schema membership or explain the 394 stable-tracked-prefix cache drops. Queue delivery, sandbox lifecycle, dependency staging, complete tool responses, usage attribution and recovery budgets remain implementation work.

Acceptance: evaluate the full assembled prompt and editable-block receipts across enabled routes using paired synthetic cases, including mixed I/we, quoted sender identity, multilingual drafts, competing memory, explicit perspective/policy changes and user-edited follow-ups. The existing six substring-based cases are a starting point, not sufficient proof. Design notes specify scope, tradeoffs, release gates and the remaining uncertainty. The selected final-window workflow review is complete; evaluation and implementation remain proposed.


### Claude reference added to the proposed prompt

The two new Claude attachments have been read fully and compared with the [official core-prompt page](https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/release-notes/system-prompts/claude-opus-5-5), whose displayed revision is 22 September 2026. [SYSTEM_PROMPT.md](SYSTEM_PROMPT.md) now also addresses concise task-sized replies, verified product capabilities, missing attachments, specific correction of mistakes, current model capabilities and forged reminder tags. [Design notes](output/platform-audit-2026-10-14/system-prompt-design.md) explain the adaptations and additional evaluation cases. For small edits, Berry must keep the complete current draft in its editable block while changing only the requested passage. Claude-specific product/policy rules were not transplanted. Proposal only: no implementation, live model validation or production deployment.


### Final-window follow-up — reply identity, qualifications and one avoidable draft repair

**H-002, confirmed reply identity error:** task `6d094655-99d9-4399-b507-71363e322b8d`, run `c9d130de-ec77-4f39-852d-823dc3128b5a` (13 October), contains an incoming manager message greeting the user by name. Berry addresses its proposed reply to that same name in both alternatives. The incoming recipient has become the reply recipient. This is one confirmed run in the completed reviews, not a population-wide rate. Current deployed rewriting instructions already protect voice; the proposed author/sender/recipient distinction and synthetic quoted-reply evaluations address an observed adherence gap. Confidence high in the error; medium in prompt changes alone fixing it across routes. Effort 0.5–1 day within H-002, with paired multi-model semantic tests, including unknown incoming sender, mixed I/we, language and explicit perspective changes.

**H-002, confirmed lost qualification:** task `466a5d0a-02d7-4748-9359-ef9e17db9210`, run `9d90b96a-9f7f-4507-9c5d-0a74ced28008`, requested catchy paragraphs. The response omits the source qualification “where issued” from the applicable measurement standard. More colourful phrasing was explicitly authorised; omission of the qualification was not. The prior project context was verified in captured same-user grounding, so project naming must not be counted as fabrication. This corroborates clause-level qualifications checks; no frequency beyond this example is inferred.

**Draft schema friction, confirmed one event in the window:** task `0512a464-fa51-406c-bfd4-ebb1e69e1691`, run `7df21a2a-15e8-476a-b63f-badf56095df9` sends `kind=other` with an empty subject to `compose_message`. Strict shared validation rejects every defined subject for non-email drafts; removing the empty string succeeds approximately 15 seconds later. `packages/shared/src/index.ts` contains the cross-field rule, while the declared tool schema exposes an optional subject string with the conditional requirement in its description. Consider normalising an empty non-email subject to omission before validation, or expressing the condition in the provider-supported schema. Preserve strict rejection of nonempty subjects and unknown fields. Expected benefit is one less repair turn for this input shape; 0.25–0.5 day, low risk if normalisation is narrow. Acceptance: empty subject/non-email accepted as omitted; nonempty/non-email rejected; email subject and full body unchanged; ownership and existing draft revisions unaffected. The event scan found one such validation failure, not evidence of a general drafting outage.

**Grounding reconciliation correction:** task `54cfafd9-7ca8-42b8-85c4-07dab576b7ac` has real earlier user evidence for the named consultant’s project involvement and design-budget management. Those details must not be labelled invented. Completion within a final delivery budget is a distinct outcome and remains unverified. Task `0512a464-fa51-406c-bfd4-ebb1e69e1691` similarly has substantial prior project context; its categorical regulatory-method claims require source/applicability checks, rather than a blanket fabrication label. Retrieved context also repeats the task’s own earlier assistant answer as project facts: evaluate deduplication and provenance under H-040. Task `1bcf3cc7-aa83-45dd-9ef9-608c85e0f08d` is not cited as a false connector refusal: captured Outlook availability was absent. These reconciliation notes supersede any provisional unsupported-addition suspicion.


### Final-window follow-up — image metadata, product help and review-coverage claims

**H-041, fresh confirmed metadata inconsistency:** task `ff7f1f05-547f-49ae-bf6f-cf0cb709cda0`, run `b946a369-763c-4ac1-87fb-cb59356e9833`, produces two images whose returned dimensions are 1024×1536 (2:3), while both tool metadata and the final answer claim 3:4. This corroborates the earlier native-image check; these two current files have not been independently downloaded, so only the metadata inconsistency is newly certified here. Return actual dimensions/aspect ratio and reject, disclose or deliberately crop/pad a supported result when the requested ratio is unavailable. Preserve exact framing unless the user authorises a transform. Acceptance: a provider fixture returning 2:3 for requested 3:4 must never report 3:4; measure ratio compliance and disclosed fallbacks. Current image path remains within the existing unresolved H-041 investigation; estimate 1–2 days plus provider-capability checks.

**H-022, confirmed unsupported product guidance:** task `e8e8e41e-2785-4de3-9853-203c9bd03aa3` contains three completed runs about the internal cost database. A scraper reaches only AESG Connect’s sign-in page. Berry acknowledges that access limit, then says the name is almost certainly locked as an import identifier and describes unverified review/approval controls. The screenshots do not establish those actions. This is not proof the cost database lacks editing; it is proof the guidance exceeds the evidence. Use versioned, permission-scoped help documentation or an actually admitted application tool; otherwise state what is visible and the specific unknown. Avoid recommending re-import without confirming duplication behavior. Effort 0.5–1 day prompt/evaluation plus 1–3 days for a maintained help source where available. Test login redirects, cropped screenshots, unknown menus and stale help versions; measure unsupported UI-action claims and follow-up burden. No other application was inspected or changed.

**H-019/H-061, confirmed overstatement of review coverage:** task `246838fb-8532-4e04-819c-50e9a16ad8b2`, run `393e0776-a944-45cf-9baf-d85cc6136896`, says it reviewed the full 428-page electrical specification after a truncated opening-range read and targeted switchboard-section extraction. Those operations support section highlights, not a full-document review. Targeted source checks confirm the unusual climate figures and key switchboard values actually occur in the supplied extraction; they are not labelled fabricated. Expose source/range/truncation receipts, preserve requested scope through recovery and report “reviewed the switchboard section” accurately. Test a large document with relevant opening data and a later contradictory clause; measure false complete-review claims and source coverage rather than runtime completion alone. Estimate 1–2 days within bounded ingestion/QA. The preceding Gemini HTTP400 is separately evidenced; its underlying provider response remains unavailable and must not be assigned a guessed cause.


### Final-window follow-up — text finalisation, recovery intent and memory provenance

Task `599e5471-b34e-4edd-97b6-b3bfbec13ac7` corroborates three existing workstreams. **H-009/H-014:** run `87582f47-fa56-4a8a-a5ae-c8e695569f6b` produces a full text answer and successful editable draft, then fails with a missing sandbox. It created no requested file. The finalisation path in `apps/worker/src/turn-runner.ts` calls tool finalisation before committing completion; `apps/worker/src/sandbox-continuity.ts` ensures the associated sandbox and scans outputs. This is a likely finalisation dependency, with high confidence in the observed failure and medium confidence in the precise internal throw site because its stack was not retained. Track output-writing intent, avoid requiring a sandbox scan for a proven text-only turn, and preserve an answer-ready state separately from artifact publication. Do not suppress real missing-output failures. Existing estimate: 1–2 days with stale-sandbox, no-output and genuine-artifact-loss integration cases.

**H-002:** a simple sentence-correction request receives three expansive alternatives and unrequested review dispositions. In later turns, a supplied PMU/EMU observation acquires BMS and technical requirements rather than receiving a restrained grammar edit. Some project specification details are genuinely grounded in this user's earlier tasks; that does not prove the current unread catalog is noncompliant or authorise a new disposition. Test faithful polishing separately from explicitly requested technical advice and verify the complete editable body.

**Recovery intent, confirmed missed request; causal contribution under investigation:** Gemini run `bd1b29ee-820a-44b7-aa72-5fa6fb970290` fails with HTTP400 before answering a new request to focus the comment on energy-meter integration with remote monitoring. Explicit Continue run `dde1aa9f-3d50-4db9-8886-048f7621fa07` completes but points back to an earlier comment and does not revise the editable draft or address that new focus. Production admission retained the correct latest user request id (`1fc32fa9-c99e-4863-a074-abdd8bb73d7a`); an incorrect admission anchor is not established here. `modelMessages` in `apps/worker/src/turn-runner.ts` adds both a system instruction to continue persisted partial output and a final synthetic user instruction to continue where the response stopped, without distinguishing a request that never received a response from a partially streamed reply. That is a plausible contributing ambiguity, not proof that the actual model request omitted the latest message. Recommend phase-aware recovery with the outstanding request, source-run lineage and completed-output receipt, while preserving exactly-once external actions. Test failure before first token, partial text, tool completion, stream failure after completion and a new correction before Continue. Measure pending-intent fulfilment and draft revision rather than only a green run state. Estimate 1–2 days within recovery work, pending request reconstruction. This is distinct from H-072's sandbox snapshot ancestry defect.

**H-040, fresh confirmed provenance failure:** the later run's captured grounding includes this task's own earlier assistant answer as project facts and new durable user preferences for numbered review comments and document-specific wording. Their cited user message contains the draft, not those preferences; the preferences reflect the assistant's advice. The earlier project grounding was reconciled to the same user's real prior task, so no cross-user leak is alleged. `apps/worker/src/memory/generator.ts` already instructs the model to reject assistant speculation, but supplies both user text and assistant final and validates returned structure/scope without a hard user-support check. Add evidence spans and provenance types, reject assistant-created personal preferences, deduplicate same-task answer echoes and retain useful source-backed project context with tenant/task permission checks. Test a user draft followed by assistant-only advice: no preference may be inferred from that advice; an explicit user preference should still persist. Estimate 1–3 days with precision and recall checks. A prompt-only rule is insufficient evidence of a fix.

- **H-001:** fresh tasks 22/25/30/35/170 corroborate failed queued corrections, predecessor completion rollback and expired questions. In task170, an SBC201→SBC801 correction is not applied before more work on the old code. Current deployment still has the insertion-order defect; prepared local fixes are not a deployed remedy.

Generic overview question reuse is intentional; the new focused-key reproduction is a code risk across fresh snapshots, not a claimed observed wrong production answer. No native-app recovery confirmation is retained. Do not report an actual off-slide defect.
- **H-068/H-069/H-070:** fresh tasks101/128/156/159/175 corroborate available-without-path imports, repeated path guessing, changed Library persistence on retry and incomplete structured paging. Task156's 25 imports become 25 reimports before many probes. One readiness handle, stable persistence intent and valid cursor envelopes should remove these repairs.

Completing workflow reading does not resolve unavailable technical telemetry.

### H-073 — CV preprocessing mistakes project-title years for dates (P1)

**Classification/frequency:** confirmed source-fidelity defect, one fresh traced workflow (task85, `20693f04-a203-4fd9-b9d9-81e4c860e025`) plus exact current production-code reproductions. Do not extrapolate its frequency to every CV.

**Observed impact:** the structured title “Dubai Metro Route 2020” becomes “Dubai Metro Route,” with2020 treated as duration; the model adds punctuation as a workaround. This is silent downstream corruption even when model extraction preserves the source.

**Root cause/current status/confidence:** high. Current enabled `cv-creator-new` content fetched through read-only SSM on2026-10-14 at 08:13Z has preprocessing SHA-256 `a9d9f67493cfe107a1ff8656a57a90ee0625f0573732093b7ceb26570269639f`. `find_duration`603–610 accepts a trailing standalone19xx/20xx; `project_from_parts`797–819 derives title duration; `extract_role_from_title`681–694 / `strip_duration`643–650 removes it even if a different explicit duration exists. Exact in-memory execution of current functions reproduces both cases and “Expo 2020”→“Expo.” Current unresolved code verified. The local checkout may contain unrelated later skill edits; current live content controls.

**Proposed fix/benefit/effort:** structured title fields are literal. Parse dates only from declared date fields or evidence-tagged source spans; preserve raw title/provenance. About0.5–1 day within CV contract work. Preserve legacy unstructured date-header parsing explicitly rather than globally disabling extraction.

**Acceptance:** Route2020, Expo2020 and WorldCup2034 with absent and conflicting explicit dates retain their names; legitimate date headings still parse. Compare names/qualifications to original source, not merely year presence. Private evidence: `group3-current-cv-contract.json`, `group3-current-skill-trace.md`, task85 workflow notes.

### H-074 — CV input requirements force unused photos and fabricated workarounds (P1)

**Classification/frequency:** confirmed current workflow-contract defects; task65 (`b05c615c-f77c-43b4-b267-75d24705a159`), task131 (`a30554f9-a110-4225-83d4-d1782140bcea`) and task118 (`44dba241-4ee4-4493-a78c-040213e5be5f`) show photo loops or blank/employer workarounds. These are examples, not a complete count of CV failures. Task131 generates five unused portrait variants; task65 also encounters repeated provider rejection.

**Root cause/current status/confidence:** high, live SSM content confirmed. Current generator SHA-256 `418a822aa3ca9c45dceeda35eddbfd7202b199ad0b94d97cf2b42a8f612d7639` exposes orientation but unconditionally requires both photo variants at145–148. Prepare helper SHA `4f7570f10d74d8402708b9a98603cc037180de04c272070845916e88b40480dd` repeats the pair requirement at385–388; photo helper SHA `bc01c2d485aacd868f849c0719cca77961cbbd13fcce56a57c79c865b5ecdbdf` requires both at39–40. Current SKILL279–280 says only Word portrait uses portrait; landscape and both PPTX variants use square.

**Contract clarification:** current schema96–113 requires a headshot; it does not declare it optional. The actual gap is inability to honor an explicit no-photo/leave-blank workflow and CLI help which does not expose conditional requirements. Validator166–170 rejects unknown employer; exact current function reproduces the rejection. White dummy photos and “Independent” employer invention are poor model recovery choices, not acceptable validation fixes.

**Proposed fix/benefit/effort:** derive required photo set from requested outputs; square-only for landscape/PPTX; explicit blank-photo mode; not-stated employer represented without assertion; atomic preparation/revision-safe resume and accurate help. About1–2 days, shared with H-055. Benefit: fewer image calls, less identity drift, fewer repeated repairs and source-faithful completion. Preserve authentic-photo and source-omission checks for workflows that genuinely require them.

**Acceptance:** requested landscape/PPTX with one square image and zero portrait-generation calls; portrait DOCX requires its appropriate input; explicit blank/photo and unknown-employer source succeeds without synthetic employer/white-image fiction. Preserve original facts and8% crop compatibility. Private evidence: `group3-current-cv-contract.json`, `group3-current-cv-prepare.json`, `group3-current-skill-trace.md`.

### H-075 — Internal Berry connector session expires while the worker keeps using it (P1)

**Classification/frequency:** confirmed current lifetime mismatch; one strongly consistent traced incident, task139 (`2e55a0ed-e62a-4502-ab67-b6970abf753f`), run `f6a54…`: seven invalid/expired calls after a long model interval, repeated sleeps and Google reconnect advice; fresh run succeeds without visible OAuth reconnection. All long runs are not counted as failures.

**Root cause/current status/confidence:** high in code, likely for this incident; exact credential/mint age not inspected. [API source](apps/api/src/connectors/connectors.service.ts:750) issues an internal signed capability with exp=now+20 minutes; verification at825 precedes Google OAuth retrieval/refresh at843 and expiry rejection is at1145 onward. [Worker source](apps/worker/src/mcp-tools.ts:205) caches turn context and its credential for the entire run without a renewal branch. Internal expiry can occur while Google's OAuth refresh token remains healthy. Fifteen minutes between successful and failed calls is not itself proof of a 20-minute token lifetime; measure age from mint/admission.

SSM code-only verification at2026-10-14T08:15:37Z: deployed API connector SHA `aae435095f30b96c307e33ea3172c32a95a7b264123d35a4d82924006ec53243`; worker MCP SHA `d189ebdfd7282a2c56f60e4622bcbb00b80015191d8bce0fb75596c20c2e5777`. Deployed modules match compiled local bytes. No credentials/secrets were read.

**Proposed fix/benefit/effort:** renew the task/user/tenant-scoped internal session for an active authorized run, refresh its server credential without discarding useful discovery state and classify INTERNAL_CONNECTOR_SESSION_EXPIRED separately from Google invalid_grant. Retry safe reads once after renewal; preserve write idempotency/outcome uncertainty. About1–2 days. Avoid broadening token lifetime or bypassing current permission checks. User should not be sent to Google reconnect for a Berry session expiry.

**Acceptance:** controlled run crosses20 minutes and a 35-minute model/human wait, then succeeds without reauth; revoked Google authorization still requires Reconnect; removed access stays blocked; no duplicated external write. Private evidence: `group3-current-connector-module-proof.json`, `group3-current-connector-trace.md`, task139 findings.

**Implementation (14 October):** past the 20-minute lease, a task-scoped session stays usable only while its run is active and the user still owns the task and belongs to the tenant (checked per call, 24-hour hard cap); Google authorization, scopes and connector policy are still checked per call. Non-renewable expiry no longer suggests reconnecting Google. Not deployed.

### H-076 — Shared-drive identity is not preserved through file discovery (P1)

**Classification/frequency:** confirmed current scope-contract limitation with observed empty-unscoped→successful-scoped search in task139; related cases86/101/114 corroborate discovery friction. Do not claim every unscoped search excludes all shared-drive files.

**Root cause/current status/confidence:** high. [Google tool definitions](apps/api/src/connectors/google-tools.ts:98) omit driveId from drive_list_children. [Search implementation](apps/api/src/connectors/google-tools.ts:279) selects corpora=drive only when driveId exists, otherwise user. supportsAllDrives does not itself carry selected drive identity. SSM2026-10-14T08:15:37Z deployed google-tools.js SHA `85c0b86769d1a83a94cad07136f8ec4b33244952dfb1c97ce219051827b25b66` matches compiled local bytes. Unresolved current limitation confirmed; not Google's OAuth expiry or the staging race.

**Proposed fix/benefit/effort:** accept/pass/infer driveId from selected file/folder ancestry, retain it through listing/pagination/search, expose incompleteSearch, and perform a bounded permission-aware fallback after an empty search. About1–2 days. Benefit: fewer empty-search loops and false missing-file reports. Tradeoffs: discovery cost and incomplete visibility; no unbounded all-drive enumeration or access broadening.

**Acceptance:** selected shared-drive file/folder is found via identical scoped query and children listing; next-page retains drive identity; incomplete search is explicit; revoked/unavailable scope stays restricted. Measure useful-file time and repeated searches. Private evidence: `group3-current-connector-module-proof.json`, `group3-current-connector-trace.md`.

**Implementation (14 October):** child listing now infers and keeps the folder's shared-drive ID, and an empty unscoped search retries once across the user's drives; results report `searchScope` and `incompleteSearch`. Not deployed.
